### The real statement + ODL comparison harness (your specific PDF)

Your PDF lives at `C:\Users\avidu\Projects\muneem\testdata\hdfc-statements\HDFC_Avi_Dullu_31072024_150007641.pdf` (Windows path). This agent workspace is a separate Linux checkout that **only** contains the safe non-sensitive `testdata/promo-emails/` corpus. Real statements are (correctly) gitignored and never present here, so I cannot directly open or process that exact file from this environment.

**This is the intended and safest design** (per `CLAUDE.md`, `DESIGN.md`, `docs/onboarding-a-bank.md`, the local_statements markers, and `muneem eval`).

#### What I set up for you

I added **`tools/evaluate_odl.py`** (included in the PR above). It is:

- Local-only and count-only (never prints values, account numbers, or narration).
- Re-uses the **exact production password resolver** (`statement_unlock.resolve_password` → keychain + your `password_rules.yaml` + prompt).
- Runs the native parser path (the same one `muneem ingest` / `muneem eval` uses) into an in-memory DB and reports rows + `reconciled`/`unreconciled` status (the oracle verdict).
- Gracefully attempts OpenDataLoader (via its documented `convert(..., format="json", password=...)` path) if installed. It parses the resulting JSON for table structures, counts tables + approximate rows, and reports status.
- Designed for exactly the "compare on this new HDFC statement" use case.

It also tells you the exact `pip install` command if ODL isn't present.

You can also continue using the existing blessed tool:

```bash
muneem eval "C:\Users\avidu\Projects\muneem\testdata\hdfc-statements\"
```

(This is the `seed → golden` harness from `docs/onboarding-a-bank.md` and `test_eval_cli.py` — it gives you a clean reconcile-rate report + per-file status.)

### Exact steps to run the comparison on your machine (copy-paste)

Do this on **your Windows machine** (the one that has the PDF and your password material).

1. **Get a trusted baseline first (do this even if you skip ODL)**

   ```powershell
   # From the muneem checkout root
   muneem eval "C:\Users\avidu\Projects\muneem\testdata\hdfc-statements\"
   ```

   Or target the single file via the new harness (native side will always work):

   ```powershell
   python -m tools.evaluate_odl "C:\Users\avidu\Projects\muneem\testdata\hdfc-statements\HDFC_Avi_Dullu_31072024_150007641.pdf"
   ```

2. **One-time setup for the ODL side** (in the same Python env you use for muneem)

   ```powershell
   pip install "opendataloader-pdf[hybrid]"
   ```

   (Requires Java 11+ — Adoptium is the usual easy install. The `[hybrid]` gives you the stronger table + OCR path that the project benchmarks as dramatically better.)

3. **Run the side-by-side on your specific statement**

   ```powershell
   python -m tools.evaluate_odl "C:\Users\avidu\Projects\muneem\testdata\hdfc-statements\HDFC_Avi_Dullu_31072024_150007641.pdf"
   ```

   Example shape of output you will see (only safe numbers):

   ```
   === HDFC_Avi_Dullu_31072024_150007641.pdf ===
   Native  : HDFCParser           rows= 42  status=reconciled    reconciled=True
   ODL     : tables= 1  rows≈ 41  status=ok               ODL JSON parsed; row count is best-effort...
   ```

4. **(Optional but recommended for best table quality on a real statement)** Run the hybrid backend in another terminal first:

   ```powershell
   opendataloader-pdf-hybrid --port 5002
   ```

   Then the script (or a small manual tweak) can target the stronger path. The harness currently uses the direct `convert` call; you can extend it with `--hybrid` style flags later if needed.

5. **Interpret the results for "precision/recall" on statements**

   For bank statements we don't have traditional token-level P/R against human labels. The project's own definition of "good" (see `validation.py`, `parser-architecture.md`, the oracle) is:

   - Rows recovered (vs. what the trusted native parser + verifier accepted on this vintage).
   - `reconciled` / bookend success (per-row balance walk + SUMMARY cross-check).
   - Whether the parse was quarantined (`unreconciled` → nothing stored) or accepted.
   - Row count delta on known-hard vintages (e.g. cases where the old `extract_tables` dropped lines).

   The harness + `muneem eval` give you exactly those signals without ever surfacing sensitive data.

### Next / how we iterate

- Run the commands above on your box and paste the (safe) output here.

  - Add a proper local test (modeled on `tests/parsers/test_local_au.py` / `test_local_axis.py`) once the comparison stabilizes.
  - Decide narrow integration (better table proposer for the generic savings path + future issuers) vs. broader changes.

After you merge the PR we can also land small follow-ups (e.g. a note in `docs/onboarding-a-bank.md` pointing at the new harness, or synthetic fixtures that exercise an ODL path if we want cloud CI coverage).

Let me know the output when you run it (or any tweaks you want to the harness first), and we'll go from there. The PR is ready for your review.
